//! Column worlds execute authored observers, including derived/count/refusal paths.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#121-column-read-intent — excluding a field from a projection must not produce a synthetic whole-row refutation.
use super::*;
fn reserve(out:&mut Campaign,cost:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(cost){return false;}
    if out.column_observer_calls+cost>16384{out.unsupported.insert("column observers capped at 16384 call/world reservations; remaining value worlds not covered".into());return false;}
    out.column_observer_calls+=cost;true
}
fn discharged(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->bool{
    inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true))
        &&!bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)
        &&!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)))
        &&!bound.programs.iter().any(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(t,_)|t!=&inv.table)&&super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&inv.table))
}
fn alternative(kind:&C,old:&Value)->Option<Value>{Some(match (kind.required(),old){
    (C::Text{max},Value::String(s)) if s.is_empty()=>if *max>0{Value::String("x".into())}else{return None},
    (C::Text{..},Value::String(s))=>Value::String(s.chars().map(|c|if c=='y'{'x'}else{'y'}).collect()),
    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|s|Value::String((*s).clone())!=*old)?.clone()),
    (C::Principal,Value::Id(s))=>Value::Id(if s==VIEWER{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),
    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(n.checked_add(1)?),
    (C::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(if n<max{n+1}else if n>min{n-1}else{return None}),
    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
    _=>return None,
})}
fn sample_rows(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,inv:&Invariant,row:&Value,presence:bool)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>{
    let column=inv.column.as_ref()?;let kind=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(column)?;
    let old=match row.field(column){
        Ok(value)=>value.clone(),
        Err(_) if kind.is_optional()&&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].defaults.get(column).is_none_or(|d|matches!(d,snapback4_core::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(Value::Null)))=>Value::Null,
        Err(_)=>return None,
    };
    let alt=if presence&&kind.is_optional(){if old==Value::Null{sample(kind.required(),0)?}else{Value::Null}}else{alternative(kind,&old)?};
    let mut out=rows.clone();
    let selected=out.get_mut(&inv.table)?.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())?;
    if let Value::Row(fields)=selected{fields.insert(column.clone(),alt);}else{return None;}
    Some(out)
}
fn same_base_world(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,primary:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,alternate:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,key:&Value)->bool{
    bound.schema.tables.iter().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()).all(|(table,definition)|{
        let empty=Vec::new();let rows=primary.get(table).unwrap_or(&empty);let other=alternate.get(table).unwrap_or(&empty);
        rows.len()==other.len()&&rows.iter().all(|old|other.iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|{
            let (Value::Row(a),Value::Row(b))=(old,new)else{return false;};
            a.keys().chain(b.keys()).filter(|c|!definition.maintained.contains_key(*c)&&!(table==&inv.table&&inv.column.as_ref()==Some(*c)&&old.field("id").ok()==Some(key))).all(|c|old.field(c).ok()==new.field(c).ok())
        }))
    })
}
fn run_pair_variant(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,presence:bool,out:&mut Campaign){
    if discharged(bound,inv){return;}
    if !reserve(out,1){return;}
    let column=inv.column.as_deref().unwrap();
    let kind=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns[column];
    let mut other=match sample_rows(bound,rows,inv,row,presence){
        Some(other)=>other,
        None if kind.contains_asset()&&fixture.is_some()=>{
            let mut other=rows.clone();
            let Some(value)=sample(kind.required(),4)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media type unsupported",inv.table,column));return;};
            if let Some(Value::Row(fields))=other.get_mut(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())){fields.insert(column.into(),value);}other
        },
        None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column pair needs a second typed value and, for media, a coupled verified fixture ledger",inv.table,column));return;},
    };
    // Both worlds receive the same verified ledger, including the alternate
    // upload. Only the protected row value changes; no fabricated media ids.
    let ledger;
    let fixture=if kind.contains_asset(){
        let Some(base)=fixture else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: media column pair requires the fixture ledger",inv.table,column));return;};
        let mut prepared=match fork(bound,base,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
        let target=other.get_mut(&inv.table).unwrap().iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
        if let Value::Row(fields)=target {
            let value=match hydrate(&mut prepared,kind,&fields[column]){Ok(v)=>v,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media upload: {why}",inv.table,column));return;}};
            fields.insert(column.into(),value);
        }
        ledger=prepared;Some(&ledger)
    }else{fixture};
    let altrow=other[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
    let observing=histories::observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
    let mut actors=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for r in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=r.field(c)&&!actors.contains(p){actors.push(p.clone());}}}}
    'actors: for viewer in &actors{
        let committed=trace.map(|(_,steps)|steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()).unwrap_or(0);
        if fixture.is_some()&&out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()<=committed){continue;}

        {
            if !reserve(out,2){return;}
            let judge=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,rows,program("$column.intent",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))};
            let Ok(mut judge)=judge else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: primary column intent world unavailable",inv.target_label()));continue;};
            let actual=match readback(&judge){Ok(rows)=>rows,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}};
            let Some(primary)=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())else{continue;};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,primary,&mut judge)!=Ok(false){continue;}
        }
        for bp in &bound.programs{
            if !reserve(out,1){return;}
            let p=&bp.program;
            let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();
            if !observing.iter().any(|table|super::super::audit::mentions(&json,table)){continue;}
            if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native{continue;}
            let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
            if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|s|json.contains(s)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque column observer declined",p.name));continue;}
            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation {
                if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation has unknown policy dependencies; disclosure-boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(|r|stability::dependencies(bound,r)).unwrap_or_default();
                if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){
                    out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation can change protected row/policy before disclosure; boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;
                }
                if stability::implicit(bound,p).iter().any(|t|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: implicit writes can change column policy before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
            }
            let args_list=histories::fixture_cases(p,rows,&actors,out);
            for args in args_list{
                if !reserve(out,12){return;}
                let execute=|alternate:bool|->Result<(serde_json::Value,BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,BTreeMap<String,Value>),String>{
                    let seeds=if alternate{&other}else{rows};
                    let mut e=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{
                        let mut e=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())?;
                        if alternate{
                            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            let Value::Row(fields)=altrow else{return Err("not a row".into());};
                            let clean=Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect());
                            if let Err(why)=e.seed_at([(inv.table.clone(),clean.clone())],NOW){
                                let immutable=t.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|fields.contains(c)))||matches!(c,Constraint::AppendOnly))||matches!(t.columns[column].required(),C::Image{once:true,..});
                                if !immutable{return Err(why.to_string());}
                                e=engine_on_store(bound,&other,Vec::new(),fixture.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
                            }
                        }e
                    }else{engine(bound,seeds,program("$column.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))?};
                    validate_fixture(bound,&mut e)?;
                    let actual=readback(&e)?;
                    let current=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).ok_or("column target is absent after validation")?;
                    if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,current,&mut e)!=Ok(false){return Err("validated column-world policy is not false".into());}
                    let actual_args=materialize_arguments(&mut e,&p.args,&args,viewer)?;
                    let observed=paired::observe(&mut e,p,viewer,actual_args.clone())?;
                    Ok((observed,actual,actual_args))
                };
                let (a,primary_rows,primary_args,b,alternate_rows,alternate_args)=match (execute(false),execute(true)){
                    (Ok((a,primary,aa)),Ok((b,alternate,ba)))=>(a,primary,aa,b,alternate,ba),
                    (Err(why),_)|(_,Err(why))=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{} / {}: declined column world: {why}",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),p.name));continue;}
                };
                out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
                if primary_args!=alternate_args{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired column arguments require a coupled asset ledger; differing materialized inputs declined",p.name));continue;}
                let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
                if !same_base_world(bound,inv,&primary_rows,&alternate_rows,key){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: committed worlds change non-protected base fields/captures or populations; reduction unavailable",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));continue;}
                if a==b{continue;}
                if !matches!((execute(false),execute(true)),(Ok((aa,pa,aa_args)),Ok((bb,pb,bb_args))) if aa==a&&bb==b&&pa==primary_rows&&pb==alternate_rows&&aa_args==primary_args&&bb_args==alternate_args){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: repeated column inputs/worlds/output are unstable or unavailable",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
                let actual_row=primary_rows[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(key)).unwrap();
                let actual_alternate=alternate_rows[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(key)).unwrap();
                out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:if fixture.is_some(){Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace}else{Reachability::SyntheticOperation},invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:primary_args,viewer:viewer.clone(),operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"column read observation".into(),row:actual_row.clone(),old:Some(actual_alternate.clone()),supporting_rows:trace.map(|(initial,_)|initial.clone()).unwrap_or_else(||primary_rows.clone()),history:trace.map(|(_,steps)|steps.to_vec()).unwrap_or_default(),rule_path:format!("authored observer depends on {}.{} while policy is false in both typed worlds",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()),scope:if fixture.is_some(){"primary observation executes on trusted fixture; alternate column is a dependency test, not an authored alternate-history claim"}else{"schema-valid synthetic worlds; authored observer, no application reachability claim"}.into(),observations:Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:alternate_rows})});
                continue 'actors;
            }
        }
    }
}
fn run_pair(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,out:&mut Campaign){
    run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,false,out);
    if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(c)).is_some_and(C::is_optional){run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,true,out);}
}
pub(super) fn generated(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))){
        if inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column clock pairs use the campaign clock only; other temporal ticks not covered",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));}
        for variant in 0..16{
            if !reserve(out,1){return;}
            let rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column snapshot: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let e=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$column.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column seed: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
            for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,None,None,out);}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){break;}
        }
    }
}
pub(super) fn fixture(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(rows)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),None,out);}
    }
}

pub(super) fn after_history(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,steps:&[histories::Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    let rows=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){

        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),Some((initial,steps)),out);}
    }
}

fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
}
fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
            }
        }
    }
}
fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
    for inv in &bound.invariants{
        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
        let judged;
        let inv=if inv.target=="always"{inv}else{
            let Some(stable)=stability::necessary(bound,p,inv)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: unknown/implicit policy changes need executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;};
            judged=stable;&judged
        };
        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1!="delete"&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: commit-end/implicit retirement is not an executed delete",p.name));}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1=="delete"{
            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(old,NOW)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
            }
        }
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()&&!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(r,NOW));
            if old==Some(row){continue;}
            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
            if !super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
            if inv.target=="always" {
                if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
            }else{
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row,prior)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false)){
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
fn role_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
    let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
    if !ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(table,_)|!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table).is_empty()){return out;}
    if !campaign.reserve(1){return Vec::new();}
    let Ok(mut judge)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return Vec::new();};
    for (table,_) in ir::write_tables(p){
        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&table);
        if !columns.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))){continue;}
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
        for row in initial.get(&table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let principal=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal).then(||row.field(c).ok()).flatten());
            let Some(principal)=principal else{continue;};
            let mut targets=Vec::new();
            for case in cases{
                let mut args=case.clone();
                for (name,kind) in &p.args{match kind{
                    ir::ArgType::Principal=>{args.insert(name.clone(),principal.clone());},
                    ir::ArgType::Ref{table:target} if target==&table=>{if let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}},_=>{}
                }}
                if !targets.contains(&args){targets.push(args);}
            }
            let mut row_cases=Vec::new();
            for actor in actors{
                let owner=columns.iter().filter_map(|c|t.written_by.get(c)).any(|a|row.field(&a.owner).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone())));
                if !campaign.reserve(1){return out;}
                let gate=judge.fixture_policy(&t.update_rule_judged(),actor,Some(row),row,NOW).ok();
                if !owner&&gate!=Some(rules::Verdict::Readable){continue;}
                for args in &targets{row_cases.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
            }
            groups.push(row_cases);
        }
    }
    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(case)=g.get(n){out.push(case.clone());}}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn initial_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
    let Ok(mut engine)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return;};
    observe(bound,&mut engine,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
}

    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}

codex
The new closure separates tables that may change from tables that may lose rows. Maintenance outputs and live read-through children still enter the possible-removal set. I’m checking the runtime retirement paths for omissions, especially where set-null or derived writes could trigger another removal.

 succeeded in 0ms:
//! Expanded helpers can write from any expression block.
// @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — legacy eligibility examines the complete expanded write footprint.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn stmts_write_tables(stmts: &[Stmt], out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    for stmt in stmts {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Insert { table, .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "insert")),
            Stmt::Update { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "update")),
            Stmt::Upsert { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "upsert")),
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, upsert, .. } => out.push((target.table.clone(), if *upsert { "upsert" } else { "update" })),
            Stmt::Delete { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "delete")),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => out.push((target.table.clone(), "delete")),
            _ => {}
        }
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr_write_tables(value, out),
            Stmt::Emit { payload: value, once, .. } => { expr_write_tables(value, out); if let Some(value) = once { expr_write_tables(value, out); } },
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                stmts_write_tables(then_body, out);
                stmts_write_tables(else_body, out);
            }
            Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
                match domain {
                    LoopDomain::Page(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
                    LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => { expr_write_tables(start, out); expr_write_tables(end_exclusive, out); }
                    LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
                    LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse) => {
                        expr_write_tables(&recurse.seed, out);
                    }
                    LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => {}
                }
                stmts_write_tables(body, out);
            }
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expr_write_tables(condition, out),
            Stmt::Check { condition, current } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                for key in &current.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { row, .. } | Stmt::Insert { row, .. } => expr_write_tables(row, out),
            Stmt::Update { key, patch, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
            }
            Stmt::Upsert { key, row, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(row, out);
            }
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, condition, .. } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
                if let Some(condition) = condition { stmts_write_tables(&condition.setup, out); expr_write_tables(&condition.value, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Delete { key, .. } => expr_write_tables(key, out),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => {}
        }
    }
}

fn scan_write_tables(scan: &Scan, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    if let Some(filter) = &scan.filter { expr_write_tables(filter, out); }
    if let Some(range) = &scan.range {
        for bound in [&range.gt, &range.gte, &range.lt, &range.lte].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(bound, out); }
    }
    for key in &scan.prefix {
        match key { IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(expr) | IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(expr) => expr_write_tables(expr, out) }
    }
    for cursor in [&scan.after, &scan.before].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(cursor, out); }
}

fn expr_write_tables(expr: &Expr, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    match expr {
        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) | Expr::Input(_) => {}
        Expr::Field { base, .. } => expr_write_tables(base, out),
        Expr::Array(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => { expr_write_tables(base, out); fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)); }
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            expr_write_tables(left, out);
            expr_write_tables(right, out);
        }
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
        Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Get(read) | Expr::Exists(read) => {
            read.key.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out));
        }
        Expr::Block { body, value } => { stmts_write_tables(body, out); expr_write_tables(value, out); }
        Expr::Scan(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            if let Some(after) = merge.position.as_ref().and_then(|position| position.after.as_ref()) { expr_write_tables(after, out); }
            for lane in &merge.lanes {
                if let Some(guard) = &lane.guard { expr_write_tables(guard, out); }
                if let Some(project) = &lane.project { expr_write_tables(project, out); }
                if let Some(value) = &lane.distinct { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                stmts_write_tables(&lane.setup, out);
                if let Some(source) = &lane.source {
                    if let Some(scan) = source.scan() { scan_write_tables(scan, out); }
                    for value in source.expressions() { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                }
                scan_write_tables(&lane.scan, out);
            }
        }
    }
}

    pub(super) fn delete_authorized(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let old = if self.schema.is_referenced(table_name) {
            self.apply_reference_actions(table_name, &old)?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — the actions may have changed the root (a maintained column mirrored from its children): remove its current image.
            let id = old.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
            self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, &id))?.unwrap_or(old)
        } else { old };
        self.delete_row(table_name, old)
    }

    /// A delete's own removal of one row, its rules already decided: receipt
    /// membership, the read-through child probe, constraints, maintains and
    /// the partition event. A referential cascade removes each row this way.
    pub(super) fn delete_row(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let table = &self.schema.tables[table_name];
        self.receipt_member_removed(table_name, &old)?;
        self.refuse_live_children(table_name,old.field("id")?)?;
        self.check_constraints(table_name, Some(&old), &old, Effect::Delete)?;
        let entries = self.maintain_scratch(self.contribution_heap(table), |runner| {
            runner.tx.delete(table_name, table, &old)
        })?;
        self.charge_source_indexes(table, Some(&old), None, entries)?;
        self.row_changed(table_name, Some(&old), None, Origin::Source)
    }

    /// Remove a row the engine itself retires (an expired one): no rule and
    /// no constraint stands between the engine and its own sweep, while
    /// aggregates and partition events apply as for any delete.
    pub(super) fn remove(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Delete)?;
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
        let entries = self.maintain_scratch(self.contribution_heap(table), |runner| {
            runner.tx.delete(table_name, table, old)
        })?;
        self.charge_source_indexes(table, Some(old), None, entries)?;
        self.row_changed(table_name, Some(old), None, Origin::Source)
    }

    /// Physically retire one row whose upper lifetime ended. Expiry bypasses
    /// the root's authored delete rule and constraints, but it does not bypass
    /// the schema's declared effects on rows that reference the root. A
    /// read-through child still retains the expired parent physically.
    // @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — expiry cleanup runs the same closure/restrict/set-null actions as an authored delete.
    pub(crate) fn retire_expired_row(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if self.physical_children(table_name, old.field("id")?)? { return Ok(false); }
        let current = if self.schema.is_referenced(table_name) {
            self.apply_reference_actions(table_name, old)?;
            // A cascade may maintain a column on the root; delete its current
            // physical image so index and cap entries match what is stored.
            let id = old.field("id")?.as_str()?;
            self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, id))?
                .unwrap_or_else(|| old.clone())
        } else {
            old.clone()
        };
        self.remove(table_name, &current)?;
        Ok(true)
    }

    /// At a program commit's end, retire every source row the commit changed
    /// or inserted whose lifetime has already ended: its final
    /// image was never live at a commit boundary, so it leaves no row for a
    /// later sweep to announce. Inside the commit the
    /// row existed as written (an insert-then-update still works). Engine
    /// bookkeeping, unmetered: the program's metering is already recorded.
    /// Server execution and device prediction both run it so a predicted
    /// final image cannot omit cascade, set-null or restrict effects. Its work,
    /// returned, stays
    /// linear in the rows retired: each aggregate row takes one net change
    /// and each audience key is scanned for a live row once. Seeds and loads
    /// ordinarily keep their rows for the sweep; row-keyed view sources use
    /// `retire_seeded_view_sources` to preserve their count-basis contract.
    // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — a row whose lifetime ended by its own commit's end never exists at a commit boundary.
    pub(crate) fn retire_expired_births(&mut self) -> Result<crate::meter::Metered, RuntimeError> {
        let Some(now) = self.context.now else { return Ok(Default::default());
        };
        // @ref LLP 3000.036.000.000#3 — an expired final image never grants a new recipient the row's id.
        // The tracker covers synced and unsynced source tables alike. Each
        // distinct entry prepaid its tree node, strings and this vector slot;
        // the rows-written ceiling bounds the commit-end walk.
        // A row that existed before the commit and ends it already expired is
        // retired too: accounted from the partitions it was in at the commit's
        // start, it revokes there and announces nothing to a partition it only
        // entered with its expiring image.
        let mut changed: Vec<(String, String)> = std::mem::take(&mut self.expiry_rows).into_iter().collect();
        if changed.is_empty() {
            return Ok(Default::default());
        }
        // The log's order must not depend on a map's iteration order.
        let order = self.schema.through_order();
        changed.sort_by_key(|(name,id)| {
            (std::cmp::Reverse(order.iter().position(|n| n == name)),id.clone(),
            )
        });
        // Server execution already reserved this bookkeeping in the source
        // writes. Device prediction instead runs the walk inside its separate
        // bounded physical-work meter: exhausting that allowance queues the
        // intent, never publishes a partial closure or refuses the write.
        let meter = if self.device_work_active { None } else {
            Some(std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, crate::meter::Meter::new(crate::meter::Ceilings::unbounded(), crate::meter::Cut::Abort)))
        };
        self.retiring = Some(Default::default());
        let retired = changed.iter().try_for_each(|(table_name, id)| {
            let Some(row) = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, id))? else { return Ok(());
            };
            let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
            if table.expired(&row, now) { self.retire_expired_row(table_name, &row).map(|_| ()) } else { Ok(()) }
        });
        let aggregates = self.retiring.take().map(|retiring| retiring.aggregates).unwrap_or_default();
        let retired = retired.and_then(|()| self.apply_retired_aggregates(aggregates));
        let work = meter.map(|meter| std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, meter).used).unwrap_or_default();
        retired.map(|()| work)
    }

    /// Seed writes historically retire only already-expired sources of

                + crate::rows::word_heap::slots::<(String, String)>(1))?;

                    *count -= 1;
                    if *count == 0 { self.created.remove(&key); }
                }

                        (table_name.to_owned(), name.clone(), canonical_json(&index_values(index, row)),
                        )
                    }).collect();
                for key in &keys { *self.created.entry(key.clone()).or_default() += 1; }
                self.created_rows.insert(owner, keys);
            }
        }

        if !self.event_free { partition::row_changed(self, table_name, old, new, origin)?; }
        #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
        if !self.event_free { partition::account_row_change::<false>(self, table_name, old, new, origin)?; }
        #[cfg(all(feature = "sqlite", feature = "web-device-check"))]
        if self.device { if !self.event_free { partition::account_row_change::<false>(self, table_name, old, new, origin)?; } }
        else if !self.event_free { partition::row_changed(self, table_name, old, new, origin)?; }
        if origin == Origin::Source {
            // @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — even a derived source that ends expired reaches commit-end retirement and its reference actions.
            if old.is_some() || new.is_some() {
                #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
                self.place_assets(table_name, table, old, new)?;
                self.maintain(table_name, old, new)?;
                if self.schema.timelines.iter().any(|t| t.maps(table_name)) { self.timeline_transition(table_name, old, new)?; }
            }
        }
        if origin != Origin::LiveAuthority {
            if let Some(image)=new { self.maintain_live(table_name,image)?; }
        }
        self.tx.track_time_audience(
            table_name,
            table,
            id,
            new,
            self.context.now.unwrap_or(i64::MIN),
            if self.event_free {
                new.is_some() && self.tx.time_audience_published(table_name, id)?
            } else {
                new.is_some_and(|row| table.live(row, self.context.now.unwrap_or(i64::MIN)))
            },
        )?;
        Ok(())
    }

fn describe(value: &Value) -> String {
    match value {
        Value::String(s) | Value::Id(s) => format!("'{s}'"),
        other => canonical_json(other),
    }
}

//! Referential actions on delete (LLP 3000.039 §3): before a delete removes
//! its row, reserve a schema-fixed charge, close over the `cascade`
//! references, refuse while a `restrict` reference still holds a closure row
//! from outside the closure, clear `set null` references outside it, and
//! remove the closure children first. The caller then removes the row itself.
// @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — closure first; probes exclude the closure; deletion dominates set null.
use crate::engine::runner::Runner;
use crate::{
    error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError},
    meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter},
    rows::{Bounds, Dir},
    schema::{Effect, references::{OnDelete, Referencing}},
    value::Value,
};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

impl Runner<'_> {
    pub(super) fn apply_reference_actions(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.reference_actions(table_name, old, false)
    }

    // Recovery has no later root-delete hook to preflight physical read-through
    // retention. Include that root before the first child action can write.
    pub(in crate::engine) fn apply_missing_reference_actions(&mut self, table_name: &str, identity: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.reference_actions(table_name, identity, true)
    }

    fn reference_actions(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: &Value, missing_root: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let schema = self.schema;
        if !schema.is_referenced(table_name) { return Ok(()); }
        let Some(price) = schema.delete_reservation(table_name) else {
            // A device without a reached child's exported price cannot charge
            // what the server charges; the server always has every price.
            if self.device { return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!("deleting {table_name} needs every referencing table's write price; queued without a local delete"))); }
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{table_name}'s delete actions have no reservation: a cascading reference lacks its cap or its child's write price (guide writes)")));
        };
        // @ref LLP 3000.039#4.2 — what the commit publishes is fixed by the schema, not by the rows the actions reach.
        let closure = schema.action_tables(table_name);
        // A public device projection carries the private column's price but
        // not the maintain that computes its value. If an action can write
        // such a source, settlement must supply the authoritative mirror.
        // @ref LLP 3000.007.001#3.7 — devices store maintained columns but never derive them.
        if self.device && closure.written.iter().any(|source| schema.write_reservation(source)
            .is_some_and(|plan| plan.price(crate::schema::reservations::COLUMNS)[3] != 0)) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!("deleting {table_name} can change a server-maintained column; queued without a local delete")));
        }
        for table in &closure.written {
            if schema.visible(table) { self.written_tables.insert(table.clone()); }
            self.action_tables.insert(table.clone());
        }
        for table in &closure.read { self.read_tables.insert(table.clone()); }
        if !closure.written.is_empty() { self.tx.widen_change_evidence(); }
        for (kind, amount) in [(MeterKind::Steps, price[0]), (MeterKind::IndexEntries, price[1]), (MeterKind::Examined, price[2]), (MeterKind::RowsWritten, price[3]), (MeterKind::OutputBytes, price[5]), (MeterKind::RuleProbes, price[6])] {
            if amount > 0 { self.meter.charge(kind, amount)?; }
        }
        // Pending event images survive the closure and subsequent statements.
        // Keep their schema-fixed reservation until the request ends.
        let events = schema.delete_event_heap(table_name).unwrap_or(0);
        self.meter.heap(events)?;
        let mark = self.meter.mark();
        self.meter.heap(price[4].saturating_sub(events))?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — draw the reservation's rows from the request's physical allowance before the first seek.
        if !self.context.is_system() { self.tx.draw(table_name, price[2])?; }
        let meter = std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort));
        // The fixed action reservation prepays the closure itself, but held-
        // group and authority proofs remain device-only bounded work. Keep the
        // active allowance reachable by nested `device_work` calls while the
        // closure runs under its prepaid unbounded meter. Recursive cascade
        // removals see the already-stored allowance and must not take it over.
        // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.4 — every device-only proof spends the separate bounded physical-work allowance.
        let owns_device_allowance = self.device_work_active && self.device_allowance.is_none();
        let mut meter = Some(meter);
        if owns_device_allowance { self.device_allowance = meter.take(); }
        let prepaid = self.tx.reference_prepaid(true);
        let action = std::mem::replace(&mut self.engine_action, true);
        let result = self.reference_closure(table_name, old, missing_root);
        self.engine_action = action;
        self.tx.reference_prepaid(prepaid);
        self.meter = if owns_device_allowance {
            self.device_allowance.take().expect("active device allowance")
        } else {
            meter.expect("prepaid action meter")
        };
        self.meter.release_heap(mark);
        result
    }

    /// Steps 3–6. The walk holds identities, never images: each child scan
    /// streams `PAGE` rows per seek, and every write rereads its row current.
    fn reference_closure(&mut self, root_table: &str, root: &Value, missing_root: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let schema = self.schema;
        let mut seen: BTreeSet<(String, String)> = BTreeSet::from([(root_table.to_owned(), root.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned())]);
        // (Vectors, not maps: the web device pays for every new collection type.)
        let mut visits: Vec<(String, Value)> = vec![(root_table.to_owned(), root.field("id")?.clone())];
        // 3. Close over cascades, breadth first. Every reached row's referencing groups must be held on a device.
        let mut next = 0;
        while next < visits.len() {
            let (table, id) = visits[next].clone();
            next += 1;
            for r in schema.referencing(&table) {
                if self.device && !through_reference(&r) { self.prove_reference_held(&r, &id)?; }
                if r.action != OnDelete::Cascade { continue; }
                self.each_child(&r, &id, true, &mut |_, child| {
                    if seen.insert((r.child.to_owned(), child.as_str()?.to_owned())) { visits.push((r.child.to_owned(), child)); }
                    Ok(true)
                })?;
            }
        }
        // 4. Restrict: every probe runs, streaming past closure rows (the root
        // included) to the first outside witness; the first occupied reference
        // in schema order is reported. One reference's probes visit each closure
        // row at most once, plus one witness per probe (LLP 3000.039 §5.1).
        let mut occupied: Option<(String, String, String)> = None;
        for (table, id) in &visits {
            for r in schema.referencing(table) {
                if r.action != OnDelete::Restrict || through_reference(&r) { continue; }
                let mut outside = false;
                self.each_child(&r, id, false, &mut |_, child| {
                    outside = !seen.contains(&(r.child.to_owned(), child.as_str()?.to_owned()));
                    Ok(!outside)
                })?;
                let candidate = (r.child.to_owned(), r.column.to_owned(), table.clone());
                if outside && occupied.as_ref().is_none_or(|o| candidate < *o) { occupied = Some(candidate); }
            }
        }
        if let Some((child, column, parent)) = occupied {
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            self.refusing_clause(&child, &format!("{column}: {parent} (on delete restrict)"));
            let via = if parent == root_table { String::new() } else { format!(", through a {parent} row this delete cascades to") };
            return Err(RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_REFERENCED", message: format!("{root_table} at {} is still referenced through {child}.{column} (on delete restrict){via}; delete or retarget those rows first, or declare the reference's action (guide writes)", self.statement_site) });
        }
        // A cascaded row can itself have a physically retained read-through
        // child. Decide that blocker before set-null writes begin, so expiry
        // can retain this root and continue without relying on transaction
        // rollback to undo a partial closure.
        for (table, id) in visits.iter().skip(if missing_root { 0 } else { 1 }) {
            if self.physical_children_outside(table, id, &seen)? {
                return Err(self.parent_has_children(table));
            }
        }
        // 5. Set null outside the closure. Each group is gathered whole before
        // any of it is cleared: a clear can recount a sibling that shares the
        // reference's index and move it behind a page cursor. Each row is then
        // reread current before its write.
        // @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — gather, then clear: no row moves out of a walk it has not finished.
        for (table, id) in &visits {
            for r in schema.referencing(table) {
                if r.action != OnDelete::SetNull { continue; }
                let mut group: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
                self.each_child(&r, id, true, &mut |_, child| {
                    let child = child.as_str()?.to_owned();
                    if !seen.contains(&(r.child.to_owned(), child.clone())) { group.push(child); }
                    Ok(true)
                })?;
                for child in group {
                    let current = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, r.child, &child))?;
                    if let Some(current) = current.filter(|row| row.field(r.column).is_ok_and(|v| v == id)) {
                        self.clear_reference(r.child, r.column, current)?;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        // 6. Delete the closure, children first, each from its current image.
        let reached: Vec<(String, String)> = visits[1..].iter().map(|(t, id)| Ok((t.clone(), id.as_str()?.to_owned()))).collect::<Result<_, RuntimeError>>()?;
        drop(visits);
        for (table, id) in deletion_order(schema, &reached) {
            let current = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, &table, &id))?;
            let Some(current) = current else { continue };
            self.delete_reached(&table, current)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Streams the ids of the live rows referencing `id` through `r` to
    /// `visit`, through its index, until `visit` answers false. A capped walk
    /// (cascade, set null) reads `PAGE` rows per seek and holds at most the
    /// reference's cap; a restrict probe reads one row per seek, so it examines
    /// nothing past its witness. Images are dropped before `visit` runs. Seeks
    /// start at the live suffix, so an expired backlog is never visited.
    fn each_child(&mut self, r: &Referencing<'_>, id: &Value, capped: bool, visit: &mut dyn FnMut(&mut Self, Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let schema = self.schema;
        let index = schema.reference_index(r.child, r.column).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{}.{} has no reference index", r.child, r.column)))?.to_owned();
        let (cap, page_rows) = if capped { (schema.reference_cap(r.child, r.column).map_or(u64::MAX, u64::from), PAGE) } else { (u64::MAX, 1) };
        // @ref LLP 3000.039#2.3 — seek on the effective deadline (a read-through child's `$deadline`), so no expired row is visited.
        let ranges = if r.table.expiry_column().is_some() && !through_reference(r) {
            let now = self.context.now.ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input("delete clock is not pinned".into()))?;
            vec![(vec![id.clone(), Value::Null], None), (vec![id.clone()], Some(Value::Int(now)))]
        } else {
            vec![(vec![id.clone()], None)]
        };
        let mut live = 0_u64;
        for (prefix, gt) in ranges {
            let mut bounds = Bounds { gt, ..Default::default() };
            loop {
                let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, r.child, &index, &prefix, &bounds, Dir::Asc, page_rows))?;
                let more = page.len() == page_rows;
                if let Some((_, key)) = page.last() { bounds.after = crate::key::decode(key).map(|v| v.into_iter().skip(prefix.len()).collect()); }
                let mut ids = Vec::with_capacity(page.len());
                for (row, _) in page {
                    if self.device {
                        // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.4 — a device predicts only over current rows: retained history queues the delete, and only a live, current (or own provisional) row is a witness.
                        // A retained, no longer current row is work the reservation does not
                        // price and a witness the device cannot judge: the delete queues.
                        if self.tx.retained_row(r.child, &row)? && !self.tx.current_row(r.child, &row)? {
                            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!("deleting meets retained {} history that references it; queued without a local delete", r.child)));
                        }
                        if self.expired(r.table, &row) || !(self.tx.current_row(r.child, &row)? || self.is_provisional(r.child, &row)?) {
                            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!("deleting needs a current image of every {} row that references it; queued without a local delete", r.child)));
                        }
                    } else if self.expired(r.table, &row) { continue; }
                    live += 1;
                    // Activation validates caps and every write keeps them; more than the cap
                    // is a store that broke its own invariant, never a hidden-row count.
                    if live > cap { return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{}.{} holds more rows than its cap for one {} row", r.child, r.column, r.column))); }
                    ids.push(row.field("id")?.clone());
                }
                for child in ids {
                    if !visit(self, child)? { return Ok(()); }
                }
                if !more { break; }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// A device predicts only over a referencing group it holds completely.
    // @ref LLP 3000.039#6.4 — the held-group proof of LLP 3000.007 §20.5, per closure row and reference.
    fn prove_reference_held(&mut self, r: &Referencing<'_>, id: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let index = self.schema.reference_index(r.child, r.column).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{}.{} has no reference index", r.child, r.column)))?.to_owned();
        let (child, key) = (r.child.to_owned(), [id.clone()]);
        // Key-decided readability, and the group itself held (a rejoined or
        // unacquired range is unknown even when the rule admits the viewer).
        if self.device_work(|runner| runner.upsert_absence_proven(&child, &index, &key))? && self.tx.current_group(&child, &index, &key)? { return Ok(()); }
        Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!("deleting needs every {} row that references it held; queued without a local delete", r.child)))
    }

    /// The engine's own write of a `set null` reference: no rule, every constraint.
    fn clear_reference(&mut self, table: &str, column: &str, old: Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let mut new = old.clone();
        if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut new { fields.insert(column.to_owned(), Value::Null); }
        let patch = crate::value::object([(column, Value::Null)]);
        let outer = std::mem::replace(&mut self.engine_action, true);
        let result = self.target_attempt(table, |runner| {
            runner.reserve_maintains(table, &runner.schema.tables[table])?;
            runner.replace_authorized(table, old, new, true, &patch, 0)
        });
        self.engine_action = outer;
        result
    }

    /// The engine's own delete of a closure row: no rule, its constraints, its
    /// `read through` children's probe, maintains and partition events.
    fn delete_reached(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.target_attempt(table_name, |runner| {
            runner.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Delete)?;
            let table = &runner.schema.tables[table_name];
            runner.reserve_maintains(table_name, table)?;
            runner.delete_row(table_name, old)
        })
    }
}

/// Rows a child scan holds per seek: the reservation's transient `4 × row`
/// (LLP 3000.039 §5.1, `Schema::delete_reservation`).
const PAGE: usize = crate::schema::references::REFERENCE_PAGE;

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::{Context, rows::{MemoryRows, Rows}, value::{Value, object}};

    #[test]
    fn prepaid_actions_keep_nested_device_proofs_bounded() {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("nested-allowance.q".into(), r#"
table parents:
  public
table children:
  parent: parents on delete cascade
  cap 1 by parent
  public
"#.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let schema: crate::Schema = crate::json_codec::from_text(
            &crate::json_codec::to_text(&bound.schema).unwrap(),
        ).unwrap();
        let rows = MemoryRows::new();
        let parent = object([("id", Value::Id("p".into()))]);
        rows.put("parents", &schema.tables["parents"], &parent, None).unwrap();
        rows.put("children", &schema.tables["children"], &object([
            ("id", Value::Id("c".into())), ("parent", Value::Id("p".into())),
        ]), None).unwrap();
        let price = schema.delete_reservation("parents").unwrap();
        let ceilings = Ceilings {
            steps: price[0], index_entries: price[1], examined: price[2],
            rows_written: price[3], heap_bytes: price[4], output_bytes: price[5],
            rule_probes: price[6],
        };
        let mut runner = Runner::new(&rows, &schema, Context::system().at(1), Meter::new(ceilings, Cut::Abort), 0);
        runner.device = true;
        // Model the commit-end walk after its outer device_work swap: the
        // bounded allowance is the active meter and no spare copy exists.
        runner.device_work_active = true;
        let error = runner.apply_reference_actions("parents", &parent).unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(&error, RuntimeError::Prediction(message) if message.contains("allowance exhausted")), "{error:?}");
        assert_eq!(runner.meter.used.steps, price[0] + 1, "the nested proof attempted its bounded step");
    }
}

fn through_reference(r: &Referencing<'_>) -> bool {
    r.table.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.reference == r.column)
}

/// The closure's rows, children before parents: a table goes first when no
/// other reached table must be removed before it (the cascade and `read
/// through` edges are acyclic by `check`), and within a table, later
/// discoveries first.
fn deletion_order(schema: &crate::schema::Schema, reached: &[(String, String)]) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
    let tables: Vec<&str> = {
        let mut seen = Vec::new();
        for (table, _) in reached { if !seen.contains(&table.as_str()) { seen.push(table.as_str()); } }
        seen
    };
    // edges: child table -> the reached tables it must be removed before
    // (its cascade parents and its `read through` parent; LLP 3000.039 §3).
    let mut edges: Vec<(&str, &str)> = Vec::new();
    for &child in &tables {
        for &parent in &tables {
            if child != parent && schema.deletion_precedes(child, parent) { edges.push((child, parent)); }
        }
    }
    let mut order: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();
    let mut remaining = tables.clone();
    while !remaining.is_empty() {
        // A table no remaining table references can go now (its referencers are gone).
        let position = remaining.iter().position(|t| !remaining.iter().any(|other| other != t && edges.contains(&(*other, *t))))
            .unwrap_or(remaining.len() - 1);
        order.push(remaining.remove(position));
    }
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(reached.len());
    for table in order {
        out.extend(reached.iter().rev().filter(|(t, _)| t == table).cloned());
    }
    out
}

//! Atomic maintenance of bounded live-parent authority copies.
// @ref LLP 3000.014.000#4-atomic-maintenance-algorithm — one source attempt owns the complete fanout reservation.
use super::{runner::Runner, write::Origin};
use crate::{
    error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError},
    meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter},
    rows::{Bounds, Dir},
    schema::{live, through::LIVE_CHILDREN},
    value::Value,
};

impl Runner<'_> {
    pub(super) fn live_read_dependency(&mut self, name: &str) {
        if let Some(edge) = self
            .schema
            .tables
            .get(name)
            .and_then(|t| t.through.as_ref())
            .filter(|t| t.live.is_some())
        {
            self.read_tables.insert(edge.parent.clone());
            self.rule_dependencies(&self.schema.tables[&edge.parent].rules.read);
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn reserve_live(&mut self, source: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if self.live_prepaid {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let plan = self.schema.write_reservation(source);
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        let plan = if self.device {
            plan
        } else {
            crate::schema::reservations::reservation(self.schema, source)
        };
        let price = plan.and_then(|p| p.live);
        if self.device && (price.is_some() || live::parent(self.schema, source)) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("live parent authority changes need atomic server maintenance; queued without predictions".into()));
        }
        if let Some(price) = price {
            self.charge_price(price.price)?;
            // Schema-selected disclosure mask, independent of live occupancy.
            self.action_tables.extend(price.children.iter().cloned());
            self.live_heap = price.price[4].saturating_sub(price.retained_heap);
            self.live_prepaid = true;
        } else if live::parent(self.schema, source) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                "live parent has no complete source reservation".into(),
            ));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    pub(super) fn maintain_live(
        &mut self,
        source: &str,
        image: &Value,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if !live::parent(self.schema, source) {
            return Ok(());
        }
        if self.device {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(
                "live parent maintenance runs on the server".into(),
            ));
        }
        if !self.live_prepaid {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Store(
                "live parent write bypassed its source reservation".into(),
            ));
        }
        let children: Vec<_> = self
            .schema
            .children(source)
            .filter(|(_, _, t)| t.live.is_some())
            .map(|(n, _, _)| n.clone())
            .collect();
        for name in children {
            self.written_tables.insert(name.clone());
            let table = &self.schema.tables[&name];
            let edge = table.through.as_ref().expect("live edge");
            let n = edge.live.expect("live bound") as usize;
            let price = live::edge_price(self.schema, &name)
                .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema("invalid live maintenance price".into()))?;
            if !self.context.is_system() {
                self.tx.draw(&name, price[2])?;
            }
            let ceiling = Ceilings {
                steps: price[0],
                index_entries: price[1],
                examined: price[2],
                rows_written: price[3],
                heap_bytes: price[4],
                output_bytes: price[5],
                rule_probes: price[6],
            };
            let saved = std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, Meter::new(ceiling, Cut::Abort));
            let prepaid = self.tx.reference_prepaid(true);
            let result = (|| {
                // Price includes retained scan, all simultaneous key vectors and
                // event bookkeeping before any private image is materialized.
                self.meter.heap(price[4])?;
                self.meter.charge(MeterKind::Examined, price[2])?;
                let id = image.field("id")?.clone();
                let found = self.metered_scan(|r, scope| {
                    r.tx.scan(
                        scope,
                        &name,
                        LIVE_CHILDREN,
                        &[id],
                        &Bounds::default(),
                        Dir::Asc,
                        n + 1,
                    )
                })?;
                if found.len() > n {
                    return Err(RuntimeError::Store(
                        "live parent physical cap invariant failed".into(),
                    ));
                }
                for (old, _) in found {
                    self.meter
                        .step(edge.fields.len() as u64 + table.columns.len() as u64 + 8)?;
                    let mut new = old.clone();
                    super::through::stamp(&self.schema.tables[source], edge, image, &mut new)?;
                    table.recompute_deadline(&mut new)?;
                    if old == new {
                        continue;
                    }
                    // All authored cells and constraint keys are unchanged.
                    // Only this origin can replace mapped engine inputs.
                    let entries = self.tx.put(&name, table, &new, Some(&old))?;
                    self.meter.charge(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
                    self.row_changed(&name, Some(&old), Some(&new), Origin::LiveAuthority)?;
                }
                Ok(())
            })();
            self.tx.reference_prepaid(prepaid);
            self.meter = saved;
            if result.is_err() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Store("live parent authority invariant or maintenance reservation failed; transaction rolled back".into()));
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
    pub(super) fn maintain_live(
        &mut self,
        source: &str,
        _image: &Value,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if live::parent(self.schema, source) {
            Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(
                "live parent maintenance runs on the server".into(),
            ))
        } else {
            Ok(())
        }
    }
}

//! Lifetime-bound rule inputs, distinct from historical recorded attribution.
// @ref LLP 3000.014#4-the-capture — captures have their parent's type and activation lifecycle.
use super::*;

pub const PARENT_DEADLINE: &str = "$parentDeadline";
pub const DEADLINE: &str = "$deadline";
pub const CHILDREN: &str = "$children";
pub const LIVE_CHILDREN: &str = "$liveChildren";

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Through {
    /// Bounded live maintenance; absence preserves captured wire bytes.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub live: Option<u32>,
    pub reference: String,
    pub parent: String,
    /// Child engine field -> immediate parent field (including its captures).
    pub fields: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub identity: String,
    pub expiring: bool,
}

impl Table {
    pub fn engine_field(&self, field: &str) -> bool {
        (self.ephemeral.is_some() && field == DEADLINE) || self.recorded.contains_key(field)
            || self.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| {
                t.fields.contains_key(field) || matches!(field, DEADLINE | PARENT_DEADLINE)
            })
    }
    /// One classification for every expiry gate, including inherited deadlines.
    pub fn is_expiring(&self) -> bool {
        self.ephemeral.is_some() || self.expire.is_some() || self.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.expiring)
    }
    pub fn expiry_column(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        if self.ephemeral.is_some() || self.through.is_some() && self.is_expiring() {
            Some(DEADLINE)
        } else {
            self.expire.as_deref()
        }
    }
    pub fn deadline(&self, row: &Value) -> Option<i64> {
        match self
            .expiry_column()
            .and_then(|field| row_image(row, self).field(field).ok().cloned())
        {
            Some(Value::Int(at)) => Some(at),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
    /// Never trust a restored effective instant; recompute from immutable capture.
    pub fn recompute_deadline(&self, row: &mut Value) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        if self.through.is_none() {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let authored = self
            .expire
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|c| match row_image(row, self).field(c) {
                Ok(Value::Int(at)) => Some(*at),
                _ => None,
            });
        let parent = match row.field(PARENT_DEADLINE)? {
            Value::Int(at) => Some(*at),
            Value::Null => None,
            _ => {
                return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Input(
                    "captured parent deadline must be a time or null".into(),
                ));
            }
        };
        let deadline = authored
            .into_iter()
            .chain(parent)
            .min()
            .map(Value::Int)
            .unwrap_or(Value::Null);
        if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = row {
            fields.insert(DEADLINE.into(), deadline);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl RuleExpr {
    pub fn map_fields(&self, map: &impl Fn(&str) -> String) -> Self {
        let term = |t: &RuleTerm| match t {
            RuleTerm::Field(f) => RuleTerm::Field(map(f)),
            _ => t.clone(),
        };
        match self {
            Self::Eq(a, b) => Self::Eq(term(a), term(b)),
            Self::Ne(a, b) => Self::Ne(term(a), term(b)),
            Self::And(xs) => Self::And(xs.iter().map(|r| r.map_fields(map)).collect()),
            Self::Or(xs) => Self::Or(xs.iter().map(|r| r.map_fields(map)).collect()),
            Self::Not(x) => Self::Not(Box::new(x.map_fields(map))),
            Self::Exists {
                table,
                index,
                key,
                provenance,
                equalities,
            } => Self::Exists {
                table: table.clone(),
                index: index.clone(),
                key: key.iter().map(term).collect(),
                provenance: *provenance,
                equalities: equalities
                    .iter()
                    .map(|(f, t)| (f.clone(), term(t)))
                    .collect(),
            },
            _ => self.clone(),
        }
    }
}

impl Schema {
    pub fn children(&self, parent: &str) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&String, &Table, &Through)> {
        self.tables.iter().filter_map(move |(name, t)| {
            t.through
                .as_ref()
                .filter(|c| c.parent == parent)
                .map(|c| (name, t, c))
        })
    }
    /// Stable ancestor order; admission rejects cycles before execution.
    pub fn through_order(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        fn depth(schema: &Schema, name: &str, seen: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> usize {
            if !seen.insert(name.into()) {
                return 0;
            }
            schema
                .table(name)
                .and_then(|t| t.through.as_ref())
                .map_or(0, |t| 1 + depth(schema, &t.parent, seen))
        }
        let mut names: Vec<_> = self.tables.keys().cloned().collect();
        names.sort_by_key(|name| (depth(self, name, &mut BTreeSet::new()), name.clone()));
        names
    }
}

pub fn validate(schema: &Schema) -> Vec<SchemaIssue> {
    let mut issues = Vec::new();
    for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
        let Some(t) = &table.through else {
            continue;
        };
        let mut refuse = |message: String| {
            issues.push(SchemaIssue {
                path: format!("tables.{name}.through"),
                message,
            })
        };
        if table.sync.is_some() && table.leave == LeavePolicy::DeliveredHistory && table.columns.contains_key("retained") {
            refuse("read through delivered history reserves retained metadata; rename the column to wasRetained".into());
        }
        let Some(parent) = schema.table(&t.parent) else {
            refuse("read through needs an ordinary application parent table".into());
            continue;
        };
        if schema.maintain_for(&t.parent).is_some()
            || schema.timelines.iter().any(|tl| tl.owns(&t.parent))
            || !schema.visible(&t.parent)
        {
            refuse(
                "read through cannot target engine-owned rows; use an ordinary application parent"
                    .into(),
            );
        }
        if table.columns.get(&t.reference)
            != Some(&ColumnType::Ref {
                table: t.parent.clone(),
            })
            || table.defaults.contains_key(&t.reference)
            || !table.immutable_fields().any(|f| f == t.reference)
        {
            refuse(format!(
                "read through needs required `{0}: {1}` without a default and `immutable {0}`",
                t.reference, t.parent
            ));
        }
        let mut fields = BTreeSet::new();
        parent.rules.read.fields(&mut fields);
        let mapped: BTreeSet<_> = t.fields.values().cloned().collect();
        if fields != mapped
            || (parent
                .rules
                .read
                .map_fields(&|f| format!("${}.{f}", t.reference))
                != table.rules.read
                && !(parent.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()
                    && table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()))
        {
            refuse("read through must preserve the parent's complete read rule and all captured inputs".into());
        }
        for (capture, field) in &t.fields {
            let kind = if field == "id" {
                Some(ColumnType::Ref {
                    table: t.parent.clone(),
                })
            } else {
                parent.columns.get(field).cloned()
            };
            if capture != &format!("${}.{field}", t.reference)
                || table.columns.get(capture) != kind.as_ref()
                || table.defaults.contains_key(capture)
                || !(field == "id"
                    || parent.engine_field(field)
                    || t.live.is_some() || parent.immutable_fields().any(|f| f == field))
            {
                refuse(format!(
                    "captured {0}.{field} must be immutable and keep its stored type; add `immutable {field}` on {0}",
                    t.parent
                ));
            }
        }
        if t.live.is_none() && parent
            .expire
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|field| !parent.immutable_fields().any(|f| f == field))
        {
            refuse("the parent's authored deadline must be immutable".into());
        }
        if t.expiring != (table.expire.is_some() || parent.is_expiring())
            || table.is_expiring() && table.expiry_index().is_none()
        {
            refuse("read through needs the inherited expiry classification and effective deadline index".into());
        }
        for field in [PARENT_DEADLINE, DEADLINE] {
            if table.columns.get(field) != Some(&ColumnType::Optional(Box::new(ColumnType::Time)))
                || table.defaults.contains_key(field)
            {
                refuse(format!(
                    "read through needs the engine-owned optional time {field}, without a default"
                ));
            }
        }
        if table.index(CHILDREN).is_none_or(|i| {
            !i.is_plain()
                || i.unique
                || i.columns() != vec![t.reference.clone(), DEADLINE.into(), "id".into()]
        }) {
            refuse("read through needs its physical-child lifetime index".into());
        }
        let mut seen = BTreeSet::from([name.as_str()]);
        let mut p = t.parent.as_str();
        loop {
            if !seen.insert(p) {
                refuse("read through cycle; break the cycle with an ordinary read rule".into());
                break;
            }
            match schema.table(p).and_then(|t| t.through.as_ref()) {
                Some(t) => p = &t.parent,
                None => break,
            }
        }
    }
    issues
}
